We further show that merely adding auxiliary modalities does not guarantee improvement on ICU mortality prediction over an EHR-only baseline. This implies that careful design of the fusion architecture and an appropriate evaluation in the clinical context are required.

</details>


### [151] [Efficient Recommendations via Graph Coarsening and Label Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22287)
*Alessandro Sbandi,Federico Siciliano,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合图粗化与多步标签传播的两阶段扩散框架，在电信领域图推荐系统中有效平衡了可扩展性、延迟与推荐质量，在真实数据集上NDCG@5最多提升50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图推荐系统面临图规模庞大带来的可扩展性和效率挑战，需要能在预测质量与计算成本之间取得平衡的技术。图粗化作为一种自适应图缩减技术，提供了构建小型但结构代表性的图的途径。

Method: 提出两阶段扩散框架：第一阶段使用领域特异性启发式方法将节点聚合为有意义的社区（图粗化），然后通过标签传播算法（LPA）或图神经网络（GNN）进行粗粒度预测；第二阶段在子图内进行第二次LPA，生成最终的用户级推荐。

Result: 在真实电信数据集上，两阶段均使用LPA时NDCG@5比全图LPA基线提升24%；第一阶段引入轻量GNN后NDCG@5提升超过50%，但训练和推理时间显著增加。

Conclusion: 图粗化驱动的方法能够在可扩展性、延迟与推荐质量之间实现最优平衡，通过详尽的消融实验量化了这些权衡。

Abstract: Graph-based recommendations are widely adopted in real-world industrial applications. However, graphs in these systems often reach a massive scale, posing notable scalability and efficiency challenges. This requires techniques that can effectively balance predictive quality with computational cost. One promising approach is graph coarsening, an adaptive graph reduction technique that offers a way to systematically construct smaller, yet structurally representative, versions of the original large-scale graphs.
  In this work, we propose a flexible two-stage diffusion framework that combines graph coarsening with multi-step label propagation in the telecommunications domain. Domain-specific heuristics are applied to first aggregate nodes into meaningful communities, reducing graph size while preserving essential business-relevant relationships. An initial diffusion process done by a Label Propagation Algorithm (LPA) or a Graph Neural Network (GNN) propagates labels across the coarsened graph to produce coarse-grained predictions. Finally, a second LPA within subgraphs generates the final recommendations for individual users.
  On a real-world telecommunications dataset, when using LPA in both stages, our method achieves up to +24% NDCG@5 over the full-graph LPA baseline. Incorporating a lightweight GNN in the first stage further boosts NDCG@5 by more than 50%, but requires substantial training and inference time. Through extensive experiments and a detailed ablation, we quantify these trade-offs and demonstrate that our coarsening-driven approach delivers an optimal balance between scalability, latency, and recommendation quality.

</details>


### [152] [Synthetic Speech, Real Signal: Paralinguistic Preservation and Cross-Lingual Augmentation via Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22304)
*Roseline Polle,Owen Parsons,George Fairs,Luis Miguel San Martin Fernandez,Cole Looney,Xiaoliang Wu,Alexandra Livia Georgescu,Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文基准测试了8种语音克隆模型在5个副语言任务上的表现，发现大多数模型能保持信号但有一定退化，并且将英语临床语音克隆到日语后，在抑郁和焦虑检测任务上优于原始跨语言迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 副语言任务（尤其是临床任务）中标注数据昂贵且某些患者群体代表性不足，数据增强方法（如语音克隆）虽被提出但以往主要评估语音可懂度和说话人相似度，而未验证是否保留了副语言信号。

Method: 对8种语音克隆模型在5个副语言任务上进行基准测试（涵盖公开和临床数据集），并将英语临床语音克隆为日语，训练模型用于检测日语真实语音中的抑郁和焦虑。

Result: 大多数语音克隆模型能保留副语言信号但存在适度退化；克隆英语临床语音到日语后，训练出的模型在抑郁和焦虑检测上优于直接的跨语言迁移。

Conclusion: 语音克隆是增强低资源语言临床语音数据的有前景方向。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation in speech is common practice for linguistic tasks like ASR, but has seen far less work for paralinguistic ones, especially clinical tasks where labelled data is expensive and some patient groups are underrepresented. Voice cloning is one such augmentation approach, but is typically evaluated on speech intelligibility (WER) or speaker similarity (SS) rather than on downstream performance, and it remains unclear whether these preserve the paralinguistic signal such tasks depend on. We benchmark eight voice cloning models on five paralinguistic tasks across public and clinical datasets, showing most preserve signal with modest degradation. We then clone English clinical speech into Japanese and find that training on cloned data outperforms raw cross-lingual transfer for depression and anxiety detection on real Japanese speech, suggesting voice cloning is a promising direction for augmenting clinical speech data in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [153] [Evolution-Aware MSA Reasoning for Subsampling via Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22314)
*Zhangzhi Xiong,Minzhang Li,Haotian Yu,Sixian Shen,Kexin Zhang,Mingrui Li,Jie Zheng,Kewei Tu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AP-REASONER，将MSA子采样重构为可控制的多目标优化问题，利用基于亲和传播的因子图推理方法，在固定预算下选择进化信息丰富的序列子集，在长程接触预测和构象集成预测任务上优于传统启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSA子采样方法（随机、基于一致性、多样性驱动等）虽是有效的启发式策略，但对子集中保留的进化信号缺乏可控性，无法灵活满足下游任务对不同进化信息的需求。

Method: 将MSA子采样建模为可控优化问题，提出AP-REASONER：基于亲和传播的因子图方法，包含进化感知的单因子、示例一致性因子和两个控制旋钮，通过消息传递进行因子图推理，输出固定预算的MSA子集。

Result: 在长程接触预测和构象集成预测任务上，AP-REASONER优于基线子采样方法，在结构敏感的下游任务中表现更优，并能可控地恢复替代蛋白质构象。

Conclusion: 将MSA子采样建模为可控优化问题具有重要价值，因子图推理可为启发式选择提供有效替代方案。

Abstract: Multiple Sequence Alignments (MSAs) provide protein language models with explicit evolutionary context, but their large depth makes subsampling unavoidable under limited token budgets. Existing strategies, including random selection, identity-based filtering, and diversity-driven sampling, are effective heuristics, yet provide limited control over the evolutionary signals retained in the subset. In this work, we recast MSA subsampling as an explicit optimization problem, where key evolutionary measures, including query identity and diversity, are treated as controllable objectives. Building on this view, we introduce AP-REASONER, an Affinity-Propagation-based factor-graph approach. With evolution-aware unary factors, exemplar-consistency factors, and two control knobs, AP-REASONER performs factor-graph reasoning through message passing to infer a fixed-budget MSA subset. Experiments on long-range contact prediction and conformational ensemble prediction show that AP-REASONER outperforms baseline subsamplers on structure-sensitive downstream tasks and enables controllable recovery of alternative protein conformations. These results highlight the value of modeling MSA subsampling as a controllable optimization problem, where factor-graph reasoning offers an effective alternative to heuristic selection.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Binary Rooftop Mapping: A Four-Class Deep Learning Framework for Green Roof Potential Assessment from Open Swiss Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22342)
*Htet Yamin Ko Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于改进深度卷积神经网络（Roofpedia）的屋顶分类框架，利用瑞士公开地理数据（航拍影像、DSM、建筑足迹），将伯尔尼市屋顶分为四类（现有绿屋顶、适合绿化屋顶、太阳能板屋顶、不适合绿化平屋顶），为城市气候适应与绿色基础设施规划提供开源、可迁移的空间信息支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅单独绘制当前绿色屋顶或具有绿化潜力的屋顶，缺乏对两者的同时识别。城市气候适应策略制定需要全面的屋顶空间信息，但缺少开源且可迁移的细分分类框架，阻碍了基于实证的绿色基础设施规划。

Method: 基于Roofpedia改进的深度卷积神经网络框架，融合高分辨率航拍影像（SWISSIMAGE）与从数字表面模型（swissSURFACE3D）提取的屋顶坡度信息，结合建筑足迹数据（swissTLM3D），对屋顶进行四分类（现有绿屋顶、适合绿化屋顶、太阳能板屋顶、不适合绿化平屋顶），全流程使用瑞士Swisstopo公开数据集。

Result: 在瑞士伯尔尼市成功实现屋顶四分类，识别出现有绿屋顶与适合绿化的屋顶，提供了可量化的绿色屋顶扩展机会，为城市规划者提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 该开源框架不仅适用于伯尔尼及其他瑞士城市，且具备全球可迁移性，能够为城市热岛缓解与绿色基础设施部署提供关键空间决策支持。

Abstract: The development of effective urban climate adaptation strategies requires comprehensive spatial information on rooftops and buildings, since such information underpins the assessment of ecosystem services provided by green infrastructure, particularly for urban heat island (UHI) mitigation. Although green roofs are widely acknowledged as a promising measure for improving urban thermal comfort, most existing research maps either current green rooftops or rooftops with greening potential, but not both. This study presents a modified deep convolutional neural network rooftop classification framework based on Roofpedia, developed by the Urban Analytics Lab at the National University of Singapore. The proposed model combines high resolution aerial imagery with rooftop slope information derived from a digital surface model and relies entirely on publicly available Swisstopo datasets: SWISSIMAGE orthophotos, swissSURFACE3D elevation data, and swissTLM3D building footprints. Applied to Bern, Switzerland, the model labels rooftops into four categories: existing green roofs, rooftops suitable for green roof installation, rooftops with solar panels, and flat rooftops unsuitable for greening. The framework identifies realistic opportunities for green roof expansion and supplies urban planners with evidence-based information for green infrastructure deployment in Bern and other Swiss cities. Because it is fully open source, the framework is transferable to cities worldwide.

</details>


### [155] [IQ-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with a Hermitian Vision Transformer for Sound Speed and Attenuation Estimation from Ultrasound IQ Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22351)
*Masashi Sode,Gianmarco Pinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IQ-JEPA，一种通过自监督预训练结合少量模拟标签来从脉冲回波数据中估计组织声速的方法。预训练在63,435个未标注的真实通道数据上进行，微调仅需10,000个模拟标签即可达到15.60 m/s的精度，相比纯监督方法标签效率提升约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始脉冲回波通道数据中恢复组织声速是一个非线性逆问题，现有学习方法虽快但依赖大量标签。模拟声速标签计算昂贵，而真实通道数据丰富但无标签。因此需要一种能同时利用两类数据的方法。

Method: 提出IQ-JEPA框架：1）使用Hermitian视觉Transformer直接处理复IQ信号，其注意力机制对恒定相位偏移等变，共轭乘积前馈网络对其不变；2）通过掩码建模进行自监督预训练（预测被掩码IQ区域的潜在表示）；3）在模拟声速图上进行微调。

Result: 在79,293个Fullwave 2.5模拟数据（2.5 MHz）上，预训练63,435个未标注样本后，10,000个标签达到15.60 m/s，相比监督训练标签效率提升约3倍（1,000标签时超4倍）。全标签下为8.71 m/s，约比InversionNet基线差2.2倍。增益随未标注预训练数据增加而增长。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练是提升标签效率的主导因素。同一编码器可迁移，冻结特征可揭示声速和衰减信息。跨分布预训练（分层模体与腹部模体）仅损失少量精度。本文被视为迈向定量超声基础模型的第一步。

Abstract: The speed of sound in tissue is a prerequisite for well-focused imaging and has diagnostic value, but recovering it from raw pulse-echo channel data is fundamentally a nonlinear inverse problem. Learned solvers are fast yet label hungry. Simulated sound-speed labels are expensive, while abundant real channel data is unlabeled. We propose IQ-JEPA to exploit both data types. An encoder is pretrained without labels to predict the latent representation of masked in-phase and quadrature (IQ) regions from visible context, then fine-tuned on simulated maps. Sound speed appears in the IQ signal as a phase difference, invariant to the constant phase offset. The encoder is a Hermitian vision transformer that operates on the complex signal directly. Its attention is equivariant to that phase and its conjugate-product feed-forward is invariant to it, so the encoder reads a quantity analogous to the one classical coherence methods use. On 79,293 Fullwave 2.5 simulations at 2.5 MHz, pretraining on the 63,435 unlabeled acquisitions reaches 15.60 m/s at 10,000 labels. This is a roughly threefold gain in label efficiency over supervised training, growing to over fourfold at 1,000 labels. It is about 2.2x below an InversionNet baseline, and 8.71 m/s at full labels. The gain still grows with more unlabeled pretraining data. Our comparisons point to self-supervision as the dominant factor. The same encoder transfers. Its frozen features expose sound speed and attenuation, and cross-distribution pretraining between layered and abdominal phantoms costs little accuracy. We see this as a first step toward a foundation model for quantitative ultrasound.

</details>


### [156] [Integrated Order Dispatching and Routing for Last-Mile Pickup via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22356)
*Yida Xu,Zhaofang Mao,Yuheng Miao,Jiaxin Zhang,Yiting Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对最后一公里取件中的订单分派与路径规划耦合问题，提出了一种集成优化框架，将学习的路径规划预言机与实时分派启发式算法相结合，在真实物流数据集上取得了优于基线方法的解质量和求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最后一公里取件中订单分派与路径规划紧密耦合，分别求解忽略了两者的相互依赖关系，而完全端到端学习在大规模变长实例上因奖励稀疏而不稳定且成本高昂。

Method: 提出集成优化框架：路径规划子问题使用动态残差图注意力网络编码器+前瞻性快递员个性化解码器；分派子问题使用路径规划预言机引导的带局部搜索的启发式算法，预言机提供近优解以选择候选快递员。

Result: 在菜鸟物流真实数据集上进行的离线评估和在线滚动时域仿真实验表明，本文方法在解质量和求解时间上均优于其他基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效支持物流公司解决实时、大规模的最后一公里取件问题。

Abstract: In recent years, the growing complexity of last-mile pickup operations has increased the need for fast and accurate decision-making on logistics platforms. This challenge is fundamentally driven by two key and tightly coupled decision-making processes: order dispatching and routing. Solving them separately overlooks their interdependence, while fully end-to-end learning can be unstable and costly on large, variable-scale instances due to sparse rewards. To solve this problem, we propose an integrated optimization framework which couples a learned routing oracle with real-time dispatching heuristics. For the routing subproblem, we develop a Dynamic-Residual Graph Attention Network encoder with a Look-Ahead Courier-Personalized decoder. For the dispatching subproblem, we develop a routing-oracle-guided dispatching heuristic with local search, where the oracle provides near-optimal solutions to select candidate couriers while retaining real-time scalability. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets from Cainiao Logistics are used to test the performance of our approach, including an offline evaluation and an online rolling-horizon simulation. The experimental results show that our approach outperforms other benchmarks regarding solution quality and solving time, indicating it can effectively support logistics companies in solving real-time and large-scale last-mile pickup problems.

</details>


### [157] [Indexing: the Beginning and the End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22361)
*Alexander Kozachinskiy,Vicente Opazo,Felipe Urrutia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"索引基元"任务，研究了RNN、Transformer（含softmax和线性注意力）以及状态空间模型（SSM）等信息瓶颈架构的表达能力。引入"因果复杂度"概念，证明低因果复杂度的架构（如低参数RNN、SSM、掩码线性注意力）在索引出现在输入末尾时无法在常数层内解决该任务；而softmax Transformer可在一层内解决，非掩码线性注意力Transformer需两层。当索引出现在开头时，小RNN可在一层内解决，其他架构需两层。所有不可能性结果都是无条件的，且适用于无限精度实数运算。实验（n≤64）与理论定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对现代深度学习架构（RNN、Transformer、SSM）在信息瓶颈问题上表达能力的统一理论比较，特别是在序列处理中"索引"这一基础原语上的表现差异尚不明确。

Method: 提出"因果复杂度"概念，对掩码架构进行理论分析；通过构造索引基元任务，在不同索引位置条件下，分析各架构能否在常数层内解决该任务，并辅以n≤64的实验验证。

Result: 1) 低因果复杂度架构（低参数RNN、SSM、掩码线性注意力）无法在常数层内解决索引在末尾的任务；2) softmax Transformer一层可解，非掩码线性注意力需两层；3) 索引在开头时，小RNN一层可解，其余需两层；4) 实验结果与理论预测定性一致。

Conclusion: 不同深度学习架构在处理索引基元这一信息瓶颈任务上存在本质的表达能力差异，因果复杂度是解释这种差异的关键概念。这些差异源于架构的注意力机制、掩码方式和序列处理能力的不同。

Abstract: We study information bottlenecks in modern deep-learning architectures -- RNNs, softmax transformers, linear-attention transformers and state-space models -- through the lens of the indexing primitive. In this primitive, the input consists of $n$ bits and one integer $i$ from $1$ to $n$ called the index, and the output equals the value of the $i$-th bit.
  We introduce causal complexity for masked architectures. We show that architectures with low causal complexity cannot solve the indexing primitive in any constant number of layers when the index appears at the end of the input. In particular, this limitation applies to low-parameter RNNs, SSMs and masked linear-attention transformers. In contrast, small softmax transformers can solve it in one layer, while non-masked linear-attention transformers can solve it in 2, which separates them from their masked counterparts. In turn, when the index appears at the beginning, we show that small RNNs are capable of solving this task in 1 layer, while all the other architectures require 2.
  All our impossibility results are unconditional and apply even to models that employ infinite-precision real arithmetic. Moreover, experiments for up to $n=64$ qualitatively align with our theory: configurations with low-parameter theoretical solutions learn the indexing task easily, while configurations that do not admit such theoretical solutions struggle to learn as the sequence length grows.

</details>


### [158] [Interior interpretability with attention rollout: contraction and propagation profiles in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22367)
*Umberto Biccari,Qian Huang,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“内部可解释性”视角，基于注意力传播（attention rollout）分析表格型Transformer的内部组织，利用Doeblin-Dobrushin收缩理论证明rollout算子在小收缩系数下趋近于秩一随机矩阵，并在代谢组学年龄预测任务中验证了收缩随深度增强的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法仅将输入变量与模型输出关联，但未解释中间层之间显式定义的交互算子如何组合。本文旨在从传播角度揭示Transformer内部的组织结构。

Method: 采用注意力传播（attention rollout）作为行随机算子，编码特征token之间的注意力介导传播；引入Doeblin-Dobrushin收缩理论，分析rollout算子的收缩系数与秩一随机矩阵逼近之间的关系。

Result: 1) 小Dobrushin系数下rollout算子趋近于秩一随机矩阵，其公共行由归一化列和决定；2) 在代谢组学年龄预测Transformer中，rollout收缩随网络深度增强；3) 训练模型与随机初始化模型的传播轮廓存在差异；4) 与PCA和GradientExplainer的SHAP近似相比，仅在高排名变量上有局部一致性，完整排名上一致性弱。

Conclusion: 注意力传播在此用作注意力介导传播的诊断工具，而非因果解释或完整Transformer的忠实归因方法。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods assign scores relating input variables to a model's output, but do not by themselves characterize how explicitly defined interaction operators compose across its intermediate layers. We introduce \emph{interior interpretability}, a propagation-based perspective on internal model organization, and instantiate it for tabular Transformers using attention rollout. We interpret rollout as a row-stochastic operator encoding attention-mediated propagation between feature tokens. By applying classical Doeblin--Dobrushin contraction theory, we show that a rollout operator with a small Dobrushin coefficient is quantitatively close to a rank-one stochastic matrix whose common row is determined by its normalized column sums. This result gives a structural interpretation to the corresponding rollout propagation profile. In Transformers trained for metabolomic age prediction, the measured rollout contraction strengthens with depth. Trained and randomly initialized models also exhibit different propagation profiles, although the present experiments do not establish the predictive relevance of individual rollout-ranked variables. Exploratory comparisons with PCA and GradientExplainer approximations to SHAP reveal localized agreement among highly ranked variables but weak agreement across complete rankings. Attention rollout is therefore used here as a diagnostic of attention-mediated propagation, not as a causal explanation or faithful attribution of the complete Transformer.

</details>


### [159] [LunarFM: A Shared Multimodal Representation of the Moon's Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22408)
*Marc Girona-Mata,Jakob Gawlikowski,Sumit Goski,Gautier Bardi de Fourtou,Valentin T. Bickel,Ben Moseley,Abigail Calzada-Diaz,Sylvester Kaczmarek,Raúl Ramos-Pollán*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LunarFM是一个多模态基础模型，通过融合来自三个月球任务的六种仪器数据（18个输入通道），学习月球表面的通用表示，支持相似性搜索、少样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归和地质单元分类等下游任务，并提供完整的数据集和预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前月球科学研究中，大量轨道遥感数据因多仪器观测异构、标签稀疏以及任务特定建模流程而碎片化，缺乏统一的、可大规模通用的月球表面表征方法，难以支撑原位资源利用和持续人类驻留等新需求。

Method: LunarFM采用多模态掩码自编码器架构，将来自三个月球任务、六种仪器的18个输入通道映射到一个共享的768维嵌入空间，实现跨模态、跨任务的多模态融合与通用表征学习。

Result: 该嵌入空间能有效支持相似性搜索、少样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归和地质单元分类等多种下游任务，并在南纬70°至北纬70°范围内提供了配准的多模态数据集和预训练模型。

Conclusion: LunarFM证明了多模态基础模型在月球科学中的可行性，为大规模月球表面分析和资源勘探提供了统一、高效的基础工具，所有代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: The renewed global focus on lunar exploration, driven by the prospect of in-situ resource utilization and a sustained human presence on the Moon, has created growing demand for accurate, large-scale characterization of the lunar surface. Although vast quantities of orbital remote-sensing data have been collected, scientific analysis and resource mapping remain fragmented by heterogeneous multiinstrument observations, sparse labels, and bespoke task-specific modelling workflows. Here we introduce LunarFM, a multimodal foundation model that learns a general representation of the lunar surface from diverse orbital measurements. LunarFM assimilates observations from six instruments across three lunar missions, mapping 18 input channels to a shared embedding space. We demonstrate that this embedding space supports a diverse range of downstream applications, including similarity search, few-shot resource mapping, mineral abundance regression, and geological unit classification, enabling efficient scientific investigation and resource-oriented analysis. We provide a machine-learning-ready dataset of co-registered multimodal observations spanning latitudes from 70°S to 70°N, a pretrained multimodal masked autoencoder, and a companion embedding dataset providing a joint 768-dimensional representation of lunar surface properties. All code and data are available at https://lunarfm.trillium.tech/

</details>


### [160] [On the Identifiability of Controlled World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22430)
*Xiangteng Zhang,Yang Guan,Bo Zhang,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在可控世界模型中的联合可辨识性理论，证明了在满足谱分离和非退化条件性动作变分条件下，JEPA的全局最小化器可正交变换识别潜在状态和受控转移，并揭示了动作覆盖不足对反事实预测的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JEPA在视觉控制和潜在空间规划中表现良好，但一个基础问题尚未解决：在非线性观测和有限条件性动作变分的行为策略下，受控潜在预测何时能同时辨识底层状态和受控动力学？状态依赖的演化和动作效应在统计上可能混淆，因此需要理论保证。

Method: 建立了高斯潜在状态和状态依赖高斯行为策略下可控世界模型的联合可辨识性理论。识别出两个策略依赖的条件：（1）谱分离决定表示可辨识性；（2）非退化条件性动作变分决定转移可辨识性。推导了近似优化下的定量界，并构建了沿弱激励动作方向的预测器扰动。

Result: 证明当两个条件同时满足时，JEPA目标的每个全局最小化器都能正交变换识别潜在状态和受控转移。反事实与策略内预测误差比等于转移可辨识性边界的倒数，揭示了有限动作覆盖的代价。实验与理论一致。

Conclusion: 本文为JEPA在世界模型中的可辨识性提供了严格的理论基础，阐明了行为策略对表示学习和动力学辨识的影响，并为实际应用中的动作覆盖设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Learning world models that infer environment dynamics from high-dimensional observations and predict outcomes under candidate actions is central to planning and control. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide a compelling framework for learning such models in representation space. Recent action-conditioned extensions perform promisingly in visual control and latent-space planning, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: when does controlled latent prediction identify both the underlying state and the controlled dynamics? This is challenging under nonlinear observations and behavior policies with limited conditional action variation, where state-dependent evolution and action effects can be statistically confounded. We establish a joint identifiability theory for controlled world models with Gaussian latent states under state-dependent Gaussian behavior policies. We identify two policy-dependent conditions: spectral separation of the predictable signal governs representation identifiability, while non-degenerate conditional action variation governs transition identifiability. We prove that when both conditions hold, every global minimizer of the JEPA objective identifies the latent state and controlled transition up to an orthogonal transformation. We further derive quantitative bounds on representation and transition identifiability under approximate optimization. Finally, we construct predictor perturbations along weakly excited action directions whose counterfactual-to-on-policy error ratio is the inverse transition-identifiability margin, revealing the cost of limited action coverage. Experiments across nonlinear observation maps and behavior policies corroborate the theory and demonstrate implications for transition identifiability, counterfactual prediction, and goal-conditioned latent planning.

</details>


### [161] [Hyperball May Not Be a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22444)
*Yihao Xiao,Jialong Sun,Zitian Gao,Zeming Wei,Chutian Wang,Ran Tao,Jiaye Teng,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Hyperball风格优化器的优势并非来自其固有的更新方向，而是来自有效步长的演化；维护恒定角速度并不能消除学习率调度问题，精心调度对发挥其潜力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperball风格优化器在大规模训练中表现优异，但其优势来源尚不清楚，本文旨在探究其内在机制。

Method: 推导了角有效学习率公式；将优化器更新分解为径向和切向分量；设计启发式实验通过修改学习率调度使不同优化器动力学相互复现。

Result: 径向分量对一步角位移的直接效果有限，不能解释MuonH与MuonWD的差异；两者差异主要源于有效步长的演化，而非Hyperball带来的优越更新方向。

Conclusion: Hyperball并非"免费的午餐"，维护恒定角速度不意味着消除了学习率调度的难题，谨慎的调度对发挥其潜力仍然至关重要。

Abstract: For scale-invariant deep networks, Hyperball-style optimizers have shown strong performance in large-scale training by fixing the norms of matrix-valued parameters and normalizing updates. However, the source of their advantage remains unclear. Starting from the angular displacement between consecutive parameter states, we derive an angular effective learning rate that accounts for the parameter-update angle, parameter norm, and update norm. We also show that the conventional norm-based measure is a special case under parameter-update orthogonality. We then decompose optimizer updates into radial and tangential components and analyze how radial updates affect one-step angular displacement. Under the training configurations considered, numerical results show that the radial component has only a limited direct effect on the angular effective learning rate. It therefore cannot explain why MuonH converges more slowly than MuonWD early in training but overtakes it later. To further isolate the underlying mechanism, we devise a heuristic experiment that modifies only the learning-rate schedule so that the dynamics of each optimizer reproduce those of the other. The results suggest that their main difference stems from the evolution of the effective step size rather than an intrinsically superior update direction induced by Hyperball. Our pretraining experiments further show that more aggressive learning-rate decay can accelerate MuonH early in training but may impair its later performance. Thus, maintaining a constant angular velocity does not eliminate the learning-rate-scheduling problem; careful scheduling remains essential to realizing the potential of Hyperball-style optimizers. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mangocrazz/hyperball-may-not-be-a-free-lunch.

</details>


### [162] [Phylogenetic signal in marine mammal and bird vocalizations captured by audio foundation models: the limited benefit of domain-specific pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22458)
*Víctor Rincón Yepes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统发育距离恢复任务，探测四个预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET）的嵌入空间中是否隐含了从未被显式训练过的进化结构。结果发现，通用基础模型（AST、CLAP）在鲸目和鸟类两个独立辐射类群中均捕捉到显著的系统发育信号，且优于领域专用模型（BirdNET、BEATs-bio），而传统MFCC特征则完全无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练音频模型是否在不经意间学习到了超越其训练标签的深层生物学结构（如进化关系），以揭示这些模型嵌入空间的归纳偏置和表征能力。

Method: 使用Mantel检验和偏Mantel检验，在32种海洋哺乳动物（1,754条录音）和20种鸟类两个独立辐射类群上，计算嵌入空间距离与系统发育距离之间的相关性。控制表征维度（PCA降维至105维）和主频等混淆因素，对比四种预训练模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET）与手工MFCC特征的表现。

Result: 基础模型在鲸目中恢复出强信号（CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74, 均p<0.001），MFCC无效（r=0.040, p=0.338）。降维和控制主频后信号依然显著。在鸟类中，通用模型（AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52）反而优于领域专用模型BirdNET和BEATs-bio（r≈0.32-0.36）。

Conclusion: 预训练音频嵌入在完全没有接受系统发育训练的情况下，自发编码了跨类群的进化信息。领域专用预训练并非必需，通用基础模型反而表现更优。这表明大规模音频预训练过程中可能涌现出对生物声学深层结构的隐含理解。

Abstract: Do learned audio embeddings encode structure that nobody told them to encode? We probe four large pretrained audio models (AST, CLAP, BEATs-bio and BirdNET) with a downstream task none of them saw during training: recovering phylogenetic distance from species vocalizations. If the geometry of the embedding space tracks the tree of life, the representation is picking up something deeper than the labels the model was optimized for.
  We run Mantel tests across two independent radiations. In 32 marine mammal species (1,754 recordings from the Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database) the foundation models recover strong phylogenetic signal within the 26 cetaceans (CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74; all p<0.001), among the highest acoustic-phylogenetic correlations reported for any taxon. Hand-crafted MFCC features (105d) find nothing (r=0.040, p=0.338). The gap survives after PCA-projecting every embedding down to 105 dimensions, so it is not an artefact of representation size. It also survives a partial Mantel test controlling for dominant frequency (partial Mantel r=0.404, keeping 97% of the variance explained), so it is not just pitch in disguise.
  We repeat the analysis on 20 bird species using the Jetz et al. (2012) phylogeny, and this time add BirdNET, a classifier trained end-to-end on around 6,000 bird species. The general-purpose foundation models recover the signal again (AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52). The unexpected result is that neither BirdNET nor the bioacoustic BEATs-bio beat them (r around 0.32 to 0.36). Matching the training domain to the target taxon does not, by itself, help. Pretrained audio embeddings carry evolutionary information across two independent radiations, and domain-specific pretraining is not required for it to emerge.

</details>


### [163] [Complexity Bounds and Approaches to Learning Projected Gradient Descent Solver Iterates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22467)
*Anjian Li,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种k-邻域数据收集策略，通过将求解器的中间迭代步纳入数据集来缓解参数优化问题中的数据稀缺问题，并从Rademacher复杂度角度推导了泛化界，最后讨论了该方法在DDDAS范式中的应用及其与GLENS方法的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺使得训练生成模型为参数化优化问题提供初始猜测变得困难，而这些优化问题通常数值求解成本高昂。作者希望通过更高效的数据收集策略来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出k-邻域数据收集策略，将收敛解的中间求解器迭代步也纳入数据集中，在不增加额外求解器运行的情况下扩充训练数据。基于Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界，并聚焦于投影梯度下降求解的单侧箱约束二次规划问题。

Result: 通过理论推导揭示了k-邻域及相关参数在泛化界中的作用，并通过两个示例展示了求解器的行为特征。

Conclusion: 该方法通过提升数据-模型-优化循环的效率，使得DDDAS范式更具能力。文章还讨论了学习求解器迭代数据的两种视角，并将其与数据高效的全局搜索方法GLENS联系起来。

Abstract: Data scarcity poses a fundamental challenge in training generative models to produce initial guesses for parametric optimization problems that are otherwise numerically expensive to solve. We therefore study a $k$-neighborhood data collection strategy that augments datasets of converged solutions with intermediate solver iterates, increasing the amount of training data without additional solver runs. To understand the benefits of this approach, we derive a generalization bound based on Rademacher complexity that reveals the role of the $k$-neighborhoods and related parameters. To achieve this result, we focus on one-sided box-constrained quadratic programs solved by projected gradient descent. We illustrate the behavior of this solver on two examples. The approach proposed in this paper enables a more capable DDDAS paradigm by improving the efficiency of the data-model-optimization loop. We finish by discussing two views of learning solver-iterate data and connect our analysis with GLENS, a new data-efficient global search method.

</details>


### [164] [\k{appa}-LoRA: Condition Numbers Reveal Which LoRA Matrices Worth Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22489)
*Jianghui Wang,Silong Yong,Francesco Orabona,Marco Canini,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出κ-LoRA方法，发现LoRA矩阵中条件数大的矩阵贡献更大，因此只更新条件数最大的前50%矩阵，在保持精度的同时减少训练参数和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA对所有矩阵均匀更新，计算成本高昂，尤其在大模型和资源受限场景下问题突出。作者首次发现不同LoRA矩阵对微调贡献不等，条件数小的矩阵贡献有限，条件数大的矩阵蕴含更丰富的信息方向。

Method: 提出κ-LoRA方法，根据矩阵条件数（最大与最小奇异值之比）排序，只选择条件数最大的前50%权重矩阵进行LoRA更新，其余矩阵保持不变。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，κ-LoRA将可训练参数减半，平均微调时间减少16.2%，内存成本降低4.5%，同时保持与标准LoRA相当的精度。条件数分析表明训练过程中所选矩阵的条件数持续下降，说明有效性源于目标性谱再平衡。

Conclusion: 不是所有LoRA矩阵都值得微调，条件数可作为衡量矩阵贡献的有效指标；κ-LoRA通过选择性更新在效率和性能间取得良好平衡，适用于大规模模型和资源受限场景。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely adopted technique for efficient neural network fine-tuning, decomposing model updates into low-rank matrices. However, LoRA remains computationally costly because it updates all matrices uniformly, regardless of their actual contribution to adaptation. This cost is especially prohibitive for large-scale models with billions of parameters and for resource-constrained settings such as edge deployment and on-device fine-tuning. We show for the first time that not all LoRA matrices are equally worth tuning: matrices with smaller condition numbers (the ratio of largest to smallest singular value) are already well-balanced across directions and contribute only marginally to adaptation, whereas matrices with larger condition numbers contain underdeveloped directions that span richer subspaces and drive most of the performance gains. This observation itself is a key contribution of our work, and it motivates a more selective approach to fine-tuning. Building on this insight, we propose \k{appa}-LoRA, a method that optimizes LoRA by focusing updates on the matrices with the largest condition numbers, which capture the most informative directions of change. By restricting LoRA updates to the top 50% of weight matrices ranked by condition number, \k{appa}-LoRA halves the trainable parameter count and correspondingly reduces compute and memory cost. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that this design cuts fine-tuning time by 16.2% on average while matching the accuracy of standard LoRA and reducing memory cost by 4.5%. Further analysis reveals that the condition numbers of the selected matrices consistently decrease over training, suggesting that \k{appa}-LoRA's effectiveness stems from targeted spectral rebalancing rather than parameter selection alone.

</details>


### [165] [Susceptible Reservoir Architectures for Regime-Conditional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22491)
*Aliaksei Kaliutau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SUSA（Susceptible Architectures）的储层设计原则用于波动率预测，并实现了两种具体架构：基于复值开链和周期储层，以及基于不同市场状态（平稳、爆发、恢复、持续压力）的条件专家模型。同时，在Qiskit中实现了开放系统q-qubit版本。模型在16个美国股票和ETF序列上测试，表现与GARCH相当，且与HARQ预测互补，集成模型平均QLIKE提升0.0116。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波动率预测主要依赖持久性和测量噪声，非线性模型难以利用剩余结构。作者希望设计一种新的储层架构，能够更好地捕捉波动率中的非线性结构，提升预测性能。

Method: 提出SUSA（Susceptible Architectures）储层设计原则，具体实现包括：基于复值开链和周期储层的两种架构，以及基于市场状态（平稳、爆发、恢复、持续压力）的条件专家模型。还实现了开放系统q-qubit版本（基于Qiskit）。模型使用AR-Ridge锚点、有界残差修正（基于QLIKE损失）。评估采用16个美国股票和ETF序列，3个不重叠的时间段（训练/验证/测试），12步输入窗口，5步预测视野。

Result: 模型与GARCH表现相当，在特定资产（IWM, XLP）上取得统计显著的QLIKE改进。模型预测与HARQ预测互补，堆叠集成模型相比最佳单一模型平均QLIKE提升0.0116，在75%的测试场景中获胜。

Conclusion: SUSA架构在波动率预测中具有竞争力，尤其是在特定资产上优于GARCH；其预测与HARQ互补，集成后效果更佳。

Abstract: Volatility forecasting is dominated by persistence and measurement noise, leaving limited residual structure for nonlinear models to exploit. We introduce Susceptible Architectures (SUSA), a reservoir-design principle for volatility forecasting, and its two concrete implementations, based on complex-valued open-chain and periodic reservoirs and regime-conditioned experts to interpret reservoir features across calm, onset, recovery, and persistent-stress states. We also implement open-system $q$-qubit counterparts in Qiskit while retaining a common AR-Ridge anchor and a bounded residual correction trained under QLIKE. We evaluate models on 16 U.S. equity and exchange-traded-fund series using three disjoint chronological training, validation, and test folds, a 12-observation input window, and a five-observation forecast horizon. The proposed models perform competitively with GARCH, achieving statistically significant QLIKE improvements for specific assets (IWM, XLP). Also models' forecasts complement HARQ-style predictions: a stacked ensemble improves mean QLIKE by 0.0116 over its strongest constituent and wins in 75% of test scenarios.

</details>


### [166] [Interpretable EEG biomarkers with bag-of-waves: Spatial and temporal waveform dictionaries for low-data regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22508)
*Athanasios Papastathopoulos-Katsaros,Steven T. Lee,Lin Yao,Ajay Thomas,Junseok Park,Matthew J. McGinley,Zhandong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 bag-of-waves 框架，通过无监督学习 EEG 波形模板（原子）将连续信号转为离散 token 序列，结合 n-gram 与多通道空间原子实现可解释分类，在三个数据集上性能媲美深度学习模型，参数少且可解释性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预定义频谱特征带有强先验偏差，深度学习与基础模型难以解释且需要大量数据与计算资源，因此需要一种低数据依赖、可解释且性能不差的 EEG 分析方法。

Method: 使用平移不变 k-means 无监督学习 EEG 波形字典（原子），将连续 EEG 转为原子 token 序列，再用 token 计数进行分类/聚类；扩展包括原子间转移的 n-gram 捕捉时序结构，以及区域/跨通道空间原子处理多通道数据。

Result: 在单通道小鼠基因型聚类（仅16只动物）、静息态痴呆分类、TUEV 六分类基准上，性能均与最先进的深度模型和基础模型相当，但参数量极少且完全可解释。

Conclusion: bag-of-waves 的主要优势在于低数据场景下表现优异，每个原子对应可观测波形，可被神经生理学家直接验证，兼具性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used to diagnose neurological conditions, but its analysis usually relies on either predefined spectral features or deep neural networks. Predefined features carry a strong bias, since they fix in advance what counts as informative, while deep neural networks and foundation models are hard to interpret and need large amounts of data and compute. We present bag-of-waves, an interpretable framework that learns a small dictionary of recurring EEG waveform templates, called atoms, using shift-invariant k-means without labels. The continuous EEG is then turned into a sequence of atom tokens, whose counts feed a simple downstream classifier or clustering step. We extend this representation in two ways: we add atom-to-atom transitions, which we call n- grams, to capture temporal structure, and we move from single-channel atoms to regional and cross-channel spatial atoms for the multichannel case. We test the method on three complementary datasets, each probing a different aspect: single-channel mouse genotype clustering with only sixteen animals (the low-data and temporal case), resting-state dementia classification (the spatial case), and the TUEV benchmark, a six-way classification of clinical EEG events (a high-data comparison against strong deep and foundation baselines). Across all three datasets, bag-of-waves achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art deep and foundation models. Yet, it operates with a fraction of the parameter count and provides full interpretability: because every atom corresponds to an inspectable waveform, the method explicitly recovers known clinical morphologies that a neurophysiologist can directly validate. Its main advantage is that it works in the low-data regime where heavier models are a poor fit.

</details>


### [167] [Dysphagia Risk Stratification in Head and Neck Cancer via Two-Stage PRO-Clinical Stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22514)
*Siyuan Zhao,Eric Ababio Anyimadu,Zachary G. Brumm,Yue Ma,Clifton David Fuller,Xinhua Zhang,G. Elisabeta Marai,Guadalupe Canahuate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于患者报告结局（PRO）与临床变量结合的预测框架，通过两阶段堆叠模型在无需视频透视成像的情况下，对头颈癌幸存者的吞咽障碍风险进行分层，验证了MDADI单项条目比总分更具预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前头颈癌治疗后吞咽障碍的评估依赖视频透视成像（CTCAE-DIGEST），但该方法需要专业设备、人员和较大患者负担，难以在常规随访中普及。患者报告结局（PRO）虽低成本易收集，但缺乏明确的临床转化框架，不清楚何时患者的自我报告症状应触发临床干预。

Method: 构建了一个单次就诊的PRO-临床预测框架，采用临床可解释的两阶段堆叠模型（two-stage stacking model），输入变量包括PRO响应（MDADI条目）和结构化临床变量，输出为吞咽功能受损风险，无需视频透视成像。

Result: 1) 个体MDADI条目包含超越总分或综合评分的预测信息；2) 可解释性分析揭示了与吞咽障碍相关的症状模式与临床风险因素；3) 该框架可作为一种无影像、实用的吞咽困难风险分层工具。

Conclusion: 结构化的PRO-临床整合方法可作为头颈癌幸存者吞咽障碍风险分层的实用、无影像替代方案，支持将其纳入常规临床监测。

Abstract: Dysphagia is a debilitating late effect of head and neck cancer (HNC) treatment, yet timely identification of at-risk patients remains challenging in survivorship care. Definitive assessment relies on videofluoroscopic imaging, as captured by the Dynamic Imaging Grade of Swallowing Toxicity (CTCAE-DIGEST), which, while validated, requires specialized equipment, trained personnel, and significant patient burden, limiting its routine use in surveillance. Patient-reported outcomes (PROs), by contrast, are low-cost, scalable, and easily collected at any clinical encounter, making them an attractive alternative signal for identifying patients who may warrant further evaluation. However, a clear clinical framework for translating PRO responses into actionable interventions is still evolving. In particular, uncertainty remains regarding when a patient's self-reported symptom burden should prompt escalation of care.
  This study addresses this gap by formulating a single-visit PRO-clinical prediction framework and introducing a clinically interpretable two-stage stacking model to predict swallowing impairment risk using PRO responses and structured clinical variables, without requiring videofluoroscopic imaging. The proposed framework quantifies the independent contributions of patient-reported symptoms and clinical factors within a unified and interpretable risk assessment model. Our findings demonstrate that individual MDADI responses contain predictive information beyond that captured by composite or global summary scores, while interpretability analyses reveal symptom patterns and clinical risk factors associated with swallowing impairment. Together, these results support the use of structured PRO-clinical integration as a practical, imaging-free approach for dysphagia risk stratification in HNC survivorship.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Safe Stabilizing Linear Feedback: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions, Optimality, and Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21794)
*Pol Mestres,Shima Sadat Mousavi,Pio Ong,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性系统与仿射安全约束，设计满足高阶控制障碍函数(HOCBF)约束且保证原点全局指数稳定的线性反馈控制器。给出了全局满足HOCBF约束的增益矩阵的充要条件，并利用CBF输出/内部动力学概念，进一步给出了该类矩阵中存在稳定增益的充要条件。最后展示了在安全与稳定约束下求解LQR和鲁棒控制问题的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CBF控制器在安全约束下广泛应用，但已有研究表明CBF控制器可能破坏系统的内部动力学稳定性。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，为线性系统同时保证安全约束与全局指数稳定性提供理论基础。

Method: 1) 刻画了全局满足HOCBF约束的线性增益矩阵的完整类及非空充要条件；2) 引入CBF输出动力学与CBF内部动力学概念，给出该类中存在稳定增益矩阵的充要条件；3) 通过代数Riccati方程(ARE)和线性矩阵不等式(LMI)等标准线性控制技术，在安全稳定增益矩阵类内求解LQR和鲁棒控制问题。

Result: 理论上完整刻画了同时满足HOCBF安全约束与全局指数稳定性的线性增益矩阵类及其存在性条件，并通过仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为线性系统在安全约束下的稳定控制器设计提供了充要条件的理论框架，并展示了如何将LQR与鲁棒控制问题纳入该框架，弥合了CBF安全性分析与经典线性控制理论之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) have become an important controller design tool for autonomous systems subject to safety constraints. Despite their popularity, recent works have shown that CBF-based controllers can destabilize the internal dynamics of the system. In this paper, we consider linear systems with affine safety constraints and design linear feedback controllers that satisfy high-order CBF (HOCBF) constraints while rendering the origin globally exponentially stable. We first characterize the exact class of all linear gain matrices that globally satisfy the HOCBF constraints, including necessary and sufficient conditions for when this class is nonempty. Then, by leveraging the recently introduced notion of CBF output dynamics and CBF internal dynamics, we provide the necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of stabilizing gain matrices within that class. Finally, we show that Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) and robust control problems can be solved while being constrained within this class of safe and stabilizing gain matrices, through standard linear control techniques such as algebraic Riccati equations (AREs) and Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs). We illustrate our results in a simulation example.

</details>


### [169] [On convergence of solutions to nonlocal optimal control problems with quasi-minimization constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21822)
*Joshua M. Siktar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类新的非局部最优控制问题，其约束条件涉及拟凸能量的极小化近似值。通过引入拟极小化约束而非全局极小化约束，解决了先前工作中由于能量极小值不唯一导致的收敛性问题，证明了控制问题解的存在性，并获得了更优的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cueto-Siktar (2026) 中采用全局极小化约束，但由于能量极小值不唯一，无法证明非局部控制问题到局部PDE约束最优控制问题的解收敛性。本文旨在通过改用拟极小化约束来克服这一局限。

Method: 将约束条件从寻找能量的全局极小化子改为寻找能量的拟极小化子（quasi-minimizers），并利用非局部分数阶梯度定义能量密度。控制问题由分数参数 s∈(0,1) 和水平参数 δ>0 参数化。

Result: 证明了此类控制问题解的存在性；获得了比先前工作更强的收敛结果，即对于依赖非局部梯度的广义代价泛函，非局部最优控制问题的极小化子收敛到对应的局部PDE约束问题。

Conclusion: 在能量极小化子不唯一的情况下，采用拟极小化约束比全局极小化约束更适合非局部最优控制问题，能够保证解的收敛性。

Abstract: This paper studies a new class of nonlocal optimal control problems where the constraints involve approximations of minimizers of quasiconvex energies. These problems are parameterized by a fractional parameter $s \in (0, 1)$ and a horizon parameter $δ> 0$, and the energy density depends on a nonlocal fractional gradient. Here, the constraint consists of finding quasi-minimizers with respect to the quasiconvex energy. Despite the fact that the energies of interest may not have unique minimizers, we may prove the existence of solutions to this class of control problems.
  The constraint in Cueto-Siktar 2026 was finding global minimizers of the energy, and this problem's main limitation was an inability to prove convergence of solutions for the nonlocal control problems to those of a corresponding local, PDE-constrained optimal control problem. While this issue arises from the lack of uniqueness of minimizers for the constraining energy, we get stronger convergence results with our new choice of constraints. Namely, we obtain convergence of minimizers for nonlocal optimal control problems that have a general cost functional depending on the nonlocal gradient.

</details>


### [170] [Stackelberg Games with a Robust Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21890)
*Juan Li,Jianfeng Zhang,Zimu Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个领导者与多个追随者组成的Stackelberg博弈问题，其中追随者求解可能存在多重均衡的Nash博弈。考虑鲁棒领导者（最坏情况假设），通过弱形式化将问题转化为零和博弈，并利用Wasserstein空间上的HJB方程进行刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在领导者-多个追随者Stackelberg博弈中，追随者的Nash博弈可能存在多重均衡，领导者面临不确定性，需要考虑最坏情况下的稳健策略。

Method: 采用弱形式化（weak formulation）将原问题转化为零和博弈问题，通过Wasserstein概率测度空间上的HJB方程进行求解与刻画。

Result: 成功将原Stackelberg博弈问题转化为零和博弈，并通过Wasserstein空间上的HJB方程给出了理论刻画。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至委托-代理问题（一个委托人、多个代理人），为处理多重均衡下的稳健策略设计提供了理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper we study a Stackelberg game with one leader and multiple followers. Given the leader's control, the followers solve a Nash game with possibly multiple equilibria. We consider a robust leader who considers the worst scenario, namely the followers would select the equilibrium worst for the leader. By using the weak formulation, the problem induces a zero sum game problem with open loop controls, which is time inconsistent. We shall characterize the last problem through an HJB equation on the Wasserstein space of probability measures. The principal-agent problem with one principal and multiple agents can be viewed as a special case of our problem, but with certain constraints.

</details>


### [171] [Strategic Inference of Adversarial Navigation Objectives for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21945)
*Ruimeng Hu,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性水下无人航行中，蓝方通过观测红方在时变流场中沿HJ最优路径运动的噪声轨迹来推断其未知目的地的理论问题。提出了连续时间似然模型、闭式局部MLE、多周期估计器及渐近CR界，揭示了流场几何通过雅可比场与海森矩阵收缩控制目标可辨识性的机制，并延伸至主动扫描设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空间变化流场中，水下航行器的路径受到洋流显著影响，如何从观测到的部分轨迹中推断敌方（红方）的意图（目的地）是一个重要的对抗性问题。现有工作缺乏针对流场几何与目的地可辨识性之间关系的理论解析。

Method: 1) 将红方建模为遵循HJ时间最优路径向未知目的地航行；2) 推导连续时间似然模型；3) 获得闭式局部极大似然估计(MLE)与常记忆多周期估计器；4) 推导渐近CR下界，分解为策略均值敏感度、参考路径敏感度和漂移敏感度，三者通过HJ特征流的雅可比场联系。

Result: 1) 提出了一种闭式局部MLE和常记忆多周期估计器；2) 证明了Fisher信息由三个敏感度分量组成，流场几何通过当前海森矩阵与雅可比场的收缩影响可辨识性；3) 在涡流和通道剪切流场景中验证了CR界预测，展示了流场几何如何决定哪些目的地可被可靠推断。

Conclusion: 流场的空间几何结构（如海森矩阵与雅可比场）是决定目的地可辨识性的核心因素，为对抗性水下目标意图推断提供了可计算的理论框架，并为主动感知（扫描设计）提供了依据。

Abstract: We study destination inference for adversarial unmanned underwater navigation in a spatially varying current field. The red vehicle is modeled as approximately following a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) time-optimal path toward an unknown destination, while a blue vehicle observes a noisy realization of that trajectory. We derive a continuous-time likelihood model for this problem and obtain a closed-form local maximum likelihood estimator, a constant-memory multi-period estimator, and an asymptotic Cramér-Rao efficiency result. The Fisher information is governed by a combined score kernel with two additive components, a policy-mean sensitivity and a reference-path sensitivity, and a multiplicative drift sensitivity whose leading geometric contribution is a contraction of the current Hessian with the Jacobi field of the HJ characteristic flow. All three sensitivities are induced by that Jacobi field, yielding a computable link between current-field geometry and destination identifiability. We further extend the framework to a sweep-dependent observation model and formulate an active sweep-design problem for the blue team. Numerical experiments in vortex and channel-shear currents validate the Cramér-Rao prediction and illustrate how current geometry determines which destinations can be reliably inferred.

</details>


### [172] [Learning from Downstream Prices: Robust Upstream Decisions with Revealed Pricing Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22027)
*Zhiqiang Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究上游企业如何利用下游零售商的最优定价决策信息（而非仅将价格视为协变量），在稀疏交易历史下构建形状约束的模糊集，将连续分布问题简化为两场景鲁棒报童问题，显著提升决策绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上游企业能观察到下游零售商的定价和销量，但无法直接获取消费者估值分布。传统需求分析将零售价格视为外生协变量，忽略了价格是下游企业基于信息优势做出的最优决策这一事实。作者希望利用价格最优性中包含的额外信息（虚拟价值条件）来改进需求估计和库存决策。

Method: 在规则消费者估值假设下，利用下游零售商最优价格揭示的虚拟价值条件与需求分位数信息，构建形状约束的模糊集。该模糊集的可行局部族为标量区间，最坏情况局部模型在端点处达到，从而将连续分布问题转化为两场景鲁棒报童问题。同时分析有界决策误差对优势的侵蚀效应。

Result: 在六种规则估值模型上，3条历史记录将平均oracle利润比从0.892提升至0.955，5条记录从0.918提升至0.986。端点方法与1001点模糊网格精度相当，但计算速度快700倍以上。

Conclusion: 下游价格应被视为战略性决策数据而非简单需求协变量。该框架允许上游企业将少量定价记录转化为可处理的决策规则，并在下游决策不可靠时提供基于分位数信息的合理回退方案。

Abstract: Problem definition. An upstream firm often observes the retail prices and sales generated by a better-informed downstream partner, but not the consumer valuation distribution that drives those decisions. Conventional demand analysis treats a retail price as a covariate. We study how the upstream firm can instead exploit the fact that the observed price was chosen optimally, particularly when the transaction history is sparse.
  Methodology/results. Under regular consumer valuations, a downstream retailer's optimal price reveals a virtual-value condition in addition to the demand quantile observed at that price. We use these signals to construct a shape-constrained ambiguity set whose feasible local family is a scalar interval. We show that the worst-case local model is attained at an endpoint, reducing a continuous distributional problem to a two-scenario robust newsvendor. We also characterize how revealed optimality tightens a quantile-only benchmark and how bounded decision errors erode this advantage. Across six regular valuation models, three (five) historical records raise the mean oracle-profit ratio from 0.892 to 0.955 (0.918 to 0.986); the endpoint method matches a 1,001-point ambiguity grid and is more than 700 times faster.
  Managerial implications. Downstream prices should be treated as strategic decision data rather than merely as demand covariates. The proposed framework allows an upstream firm to convert a small number of pricing records into a tractable decision rule, while providing a principled fallback to quantile-only information when downstream decisions are unreliable.

</details>


### [173] [Designing Eco-Efficient Biowaste Valorisation Systems: Effects of Transport Distance and Facility Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22092)
*Christian Ottini,Gwenola Yannou-Le Bris,Sandra Domenek,Felipe Buendia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究建立了统一的技术经济与生命周期评估框架，量化了运输距离与处理规模对生物废弃物处理系统（堆肥与厌氧消化）环境经济性能的非线性影响，发现零距离堆肥综合效益最佳，并为基础设施规划提供了量化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲分类收集生物废弃物日益普及，但分散式与集中式基础设施的平衡、技术选择（堆肥与厌氧消化）如何受运输距离、设施规模等因素影响尚不明确，关键阈值与规模-距离交互作用缺乏系统量化。

Method: 建立统一的技术经济与生命周期评估框架，评估三种规模的堆肥和厌氧消化设施在不同运输距离下的表现，并与当地焚烧对比；运输负荷使用车辆LCA和电动/燃气/柴油重型卡车实际数据建模。

Result: 发现规模与运输之间存在非线性权衡，零距离堆肥综合影响最低；45种配置在经济和环境上均优于当地焚烧，111种虽成本更高但环境更优。

Conclusion: 为生物废弃物处理基础设施规划提供了定量依据，揭示了不同技术选择下环境与经济性能的复杂权衡关系。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of separate biowaste collection across Europe raises questions regarding environmentally and economically optimal treatment strategies. From this perspective, the balance between decentralised and centralised infrastructure is a potential lever to consider. The choice of technologies is another. Composting and anaerobic digestion (AD) offer viable circular bioeconomy pathways, but their performance depends on transport distance, facility size, seasonal waste fluctuations, and technological efficiency. Threshold distances and scale-dependent interactions affecting the environmental and economic performance of treatment plants remain poorly characterised. This study develops a unified techno-economic and life cycle assessment framework to quantify how transport constraints and treatment capacities shape biowaste valorisation systems. Three facility scales for composting and AD are evaluated across a range of transport distances and compared with local incineration in terms of environmental and economic performance. Transport burdens are modelled using vehicle life cycle assessment (LCA) and realistic heavy-duty truck data for electric-, gas-, and diesel-powered vehicles. Results reveal non-linear trade-offs between scale and transport, with zero-distance composting yielding the lowest combined impacts. Across all scenarios, 45 configurations outperform local incineration economically and environmentally, while 111 remain environmentally preferable despite higher costs, thus providing quantitative guidance for infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [174] [Non-Negative Conjugate Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22121)
*Thomas Schmelzer,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出非负共轭梯度法（Non-negative CG），一种在Krylov子空间框架下求解带非负约束及等式约束的二次规划问题的算法，通过主对偶有效集循环与块主旋转终止准则，保证有限步收敛到全局最优解，并支持无矩阵实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轭梯度法高效求解SPD系统但不支持非负约束，而实际应用中很多问题要求解非负。现有方法如Lawson–Hanson或内点法在稠密问题上效率不足，需要一个兼具CG效率与约束处理能力的方法。

Method: 将CG嵌入主对偶有效集循环：每步对自由变量求解无约束系统，丢弃变负变量，重新接纳负简化梯度达界的约束变量，重启CG。切换遵循LCP的主旋转规则，终止采用Júdice–Pires块主旋转构造加Bland最小索引回退。在非精确Krylov内层求解中，将CG残差与决策边界关联，保证近似循环与精确循环做出相同的原始/对偶符号决策。

Result: 对A=M^⊤M只需两次M乘积，无O(n²)存储，保持O(√κ)收敛率；正则化/Tikhonov分裂确保P-矩阵性质。合成SPD问题最多6步外层循环达到最优，稠密实例上比Lawson–Hanson和内点法快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出了一种融合CG效率与约束处理能力的可证明收敛算法，支持无矩阵实现，在约束二次规划领域具有显著的理论与实用价值。

Abstract: The conjugate gradient method (CG) solves a symmetric positive definite (SPD) system $Ax=b$, but does not respect $x\geq0$. We develop non-negative conjugate gradients, a solver for the bound-constrained quadratic $\min_x \tfrac12 x^\top A x - b^\top x$ subject to $x\geq0$, $Bx=c$, $A\succ0$, with $Bx=c$ present only in the equality-augmented variant. The solver wraps CG in a primal-dual active-set loop: each step solves the unconstrained system over the free variables, drops variables that turn negative, re-admits any bound variable with negative reduced gradient, and restarts CG. These toggles are the principal pivots of the linear complementarity problem $(A,-b)$, well defined since $A$ is a $P$-matrix. Termination uses the block-principal-pivoting construction of Júdice and Pires, with a least-index Bland fallback, reaching the unique global minimiser in finitely many outer steps. Our contribution carries this guarantee to the inexact, matrix-free regime the Krylov inner solve requires: once CG's residual is tied to the decision margin, the approximate loop makes the same primal and dual sign decisions as the exact one, and a direct factorisation may replace CG unchanged. Each inner solve is matrix-free: for $A=M^\top M$, the operator $v\mapsto M^\top(Mv)$ needs two products with $M$, no $O(n^2)$ storage, and retains CG's $O(\sqrtκ)$ rate, $κ=κ(A)$. Regularisation or preconditioning that compresses the spectrum lowers the iteration count; a ridge/Tikhonov split $A\mapsto(1-α)A+αR^\top R$ also secures the needed $P$-matrix property. A loop-free projected-gradient reference method, analysed alongside, converges at the slower $O(κ)$ rate. On synthetic SPD problems the loop reaches the planted optimum in at most 6 outer steps, and with a direct free-set solve outpaces Lawson--Hanson and an interior-point solver by an order of magnitude on dense instances.

</details>


### [175] [Finite-Time Stabilization of Linear Systems via Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22191)
*Weihao Weng,Yacine Chitour,Paolo Mason*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于线性系统有限时间镇定的最优控制框架，通过构建合适的积分代价函数推导出非线性控制器，验证了其有限时间稳定性，并分析了收敛时间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性系统镇定方法大多仅实现渐近稳定，缺乏对有限时间收敛的严格保证。本文旨在通过最优控制理论设计可实现有限时间镇定的控制器。

Method: 构建特定形式的积分代价函数，利用最优性原理导出非线性控制律；推导对应的HJB方程并分析其正则性；通过数值仿真验证理论结果。

Result: 成功推导出一类保证有限时间稳定的非线性控制器，HJB方程的正则性得到分析，数值仿真验证了方法的有效性，并提供了收敛时间的估计方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优控制框架能够有效实现线性系统的有限时间镇定，HJB方程的求解与分析为后续研究提供了理论基础，收敛时间估计为实际应用提供了参考。

Abstract: This paper presents an optimal control framework for achieving finite-time stabilization of linear systems. By introducing a suitably constructed integral cost function, we derive a new class of nonlinear controllers that guarantee finite-time stability through the application of the optimality principle. The relationship between the resulting optimal control law and the associated value function is analyzed, leading to the derivation of a Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation and the study of its regularity properties. Numerical simulations validate the theoretical findings and illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Furthermore, a discussion on estimating the convergence time is provided.

</details>


### [176] [Explicit Iteration Complexity of Exact Data-Driven Inverse Optimization for Integer Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22263)
*Akira Kitaoka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据驱动逆优化问题（DDIOP），特别是整数线性规划（ILP）场景，给出了投影次梯度下降法实现精确一致性的显式迭代次数上界，该上界是样本数、特征维度、特征范围和约束系数矩阵结构的多项式函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已证明逆ILP问题可通过梯度方法在有限次迭代内精确求解，但迭代次数的上界依赖于一个问题相关的几何常数γ，而该常数无法被显式表达为问题规模的函数，导致实际应用中无法预先估计所需计算量。

Method: 本文通过分析整数线性规划的正向问题结构，将投影次梯度下降法应用于次优性损失函数，利用约束系数矩阵的结构特性、特征取值范围等可计算的量，推导出γ的下界，从而将迭代次数上界转化为样本数、特征维度、特征范围和约束系数矩阵结构的显式多项式表达式。

Result: 给出了投影次梯度下降法在逆ILP问题中实现精确一致性所需的迭代次数上界，该上界是样本数、特征维度、特征范围、约束系数矩阵结构的显式函数（多项式因子），并包含了权重集直径、步长参数和次优性损失Lipschitz常数等基础常数。

Conclusion: 本文首次将逆ILP问题中梯度方法的迭代次数上界从依赖于未知几何常数的形式，转化为完全显式的、关于问题规模的多项式表达式，为实际应用中的计算量预估提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A data-driven inverse optimization problem (DDIOP) is the problem of estimating the objective-function parameters (weights) that explain observed optimal-solution data, and it arises in many applications, including integer linear programming (ILP). It is known that, by applying gradient-based optimization methods to the suboptimality loss, the inverse optimization of ILPs can be solved exactly within finitely many oracle iterations, and that the required number of iterations is bounded as $T=O(1/γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})^2)$ in terms of a problem-dependent geometric constant $γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})$. However, no means of bounding $γ(\ell_{\mathrm{sub}})$ from below as a function of the problem size has been available, and hence the number of iterations could not be given as an explicit function of the problem size. We therefore give, when the forward problem is an integer linear program (ILP), the number of iterations sufficient for projected subgradient descent applied to the suboptimality loss to achieve exact consistency with the observed data, as a fully explicit function of the number of samples, the dimension of the features, the ranges of the features, and the structure of the constraint coefficient matrix, up to polynomial factors in the basic constants (the diameter of the weight set, the step-size parameter, and the Lipschitz constant of the suboptimality loss).

</details>


### [177] [Homogeneous Self-Dual Embedding via Perspective Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22278)
*Goran Banjac*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义的自对偶嵌入模型，将经典的同质自对偶嵌入推广到求解两个凸函数和的最小化问题，并利用Douglas-Rachford算法求解该嵌入，从而恢复出求解二次锥规划的特殊情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的用于锥优化的同质自对偶嵌入模型推广到更一般的凸优化问题（最小化两个正常下半连续凸函数的和），以统一处理原始-对偶解的存在性和不可行性判定。

Method: 提出一个新的自对偶嵌入模型，该模型利用原始函数及其共轭函数的透视表示为一个不等式；然后使用Douglas-Rachford算法求解该嵌入，并通过利用问题结构实现高效实现。

Result: 提出的算法恢复出求解二次锥规划（QCP）的现有方法作为特例；并在非光滑目标函数和非锥约束的凸优化问题上验证了算法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该广义自对偶嵌入框架为更广泛的凸优化问题提供了统一的求解范式，且实际算法在非锥约束问题上表现良好。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the well-known homogeneous self-dual embedding model, which is widely used in conic optimization. The new embedding applies to a problem of minimizing the sum of two proper lower-semicontinuous convex functions and can be represented as a single inequality that uses perspectives of these functions and of their conjugates. A solution to the proposed embedding encodes a primal-dual solution to the original problem when available, or an infeasibility certificate otherwise. We then use the Douglas-Rachford algorithm to find a solution to the embedding and discuss its efficient implementation by exploiting the problem structure. The resulting algorithm recovers an existing method for solving quadratic cone programs as a special case. We demonstrate the generality and effectiveness of the algorithm on a class of convex optimization problems with non-smooth objective function and non-conic constraints.

</details>


### [178] [Langevin for Nonconvex Optimization: Exact, Inexact and Zeroth-Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22353)
*Emanuele Naldi,Marco Rando,Lorenzo Rosasco,Silvia Villa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Langevin的非凸优化方法，通过相对熵直接转化为目标值误差（而非中间Wasserstein界），给出了精确梯度ULA和非精确梯度ULA（包括随机梯度和零阶估计器）的非渐近期望过剩风险界，首次为零阶ULA提供了非渐近全局非凸优化复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸Langevin优化分析通常依赖中间Wasserstein界，导致对Log-Sobolev常数的依赖不理想（该常数在非凸问题中可能随逆温度和维度指数增长）。作者希望绕开这一瓶颈，直接建立相对熵与目标函数值误差之间的联系，从而得到更优的依赖关系。

Method: 提出加权Csiszár-Kullback-Pinsker不等式与指数矩估计，实现从相对熵到目标值误差的直接转化。分析分为两部分：(1) 精确梯度ULA，给出显式界；(2) 非精确梯度ULA，允许有偏/随机梯度替代（均方误差随状态最多二次增长），涵盖随机梯度和零阶有限差分估计（高斯与球形）。

Result: 导出精确ULA和非精确ULA在非凸条件下的期望过剩风险显式界，依赖逆温度、维度、步长、光滑性、耗散性及Log-Sobolev常数。为零阶ULA首次建立了非渐近全局非凸优化复杂度界，并通过数值实验验证了所提零阶Langevin方案的性能。

Conclusion: 所提的直接相对熵-目标误差技术显著改善了非凸Langevin优化的理论界，尤其为难以获取梯度的问题（零阶优化）提供了严格的复杂度保证，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We study Langevin-based methods for non-convex optimization under smoothness and dissipativity assumptions. Our focus is on obtaining non-asymptotic bounds for the expected excess risk rather than sampling guarantees for the full target distribution. The key ingredient of our analysis is a direct passage from relative entropy to objective-value error, based on a weighted Csiszár--Kullback--Pinsker inequality and exponential-moment estimates. This avoids intermediate Wasserstein bounds and yields sharper dependence on the Log-Sobolev constant, a quantity that may scale exponentially with the inverse temperature and the dimension in non-convex problems. We first analyze the Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm with exact gradients and derive explicit bounds on $\mathbb{E}[F(x_k)]-\min F$ in terms of the inverse temperature, dimension, stepsize, smoothness and dissipativity parameters, and the Log-Sobolev constant. We then extend the result to an inexact-gradient version of ULA, allowing for biased and stochastic gradient surrogates whose mean-square error grows at most quadratically in the state. This framework covers stochastic gradients and zeroth-order estimators based only on function evaluations. In particular, we show that both Gaussian and spherical finite-difference estimators fit into the inexact-ULA theory and obtain explicit function-evaluation complexity bounds for zeroth-order Langevin optimization. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first non-asymptotic global non-convex optimization complexity bounds for zeroth-order ULA. We also provide numerical experiments illustrating the behavior of the proposed zeroth-order Langevin schemes.

</details>


### [179] [Data Driven Modeling of Nonlinear Dynamics in a Rotating Detonation Combustor via Finite Dimensional Approximations of the Koopman Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22457)
*David Oexle,Tobias Breiten,Myles D. Bohon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用动态模态分解（DMD）和Koopman算子理论，通过高速视频数据对旋转爆震燃烧室（RDC）中多种运行模式的非线性动力学进行建模与分析，引入时间延迟嵌入克服标准DMD局限，并提出了传感器噪声抑制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转爆震燃烧室（RDC）具有提高推进与发电效率的潜力，但其动力学受连续传播的爆震波控制，存在多种运行模式（包括反向旋转波的非线性相互作用及驻波模式），需要有效的全局线性化分析方法来理解这些复杂动力学行为。

Method: 基于Koopman算子理论，利用动态模态分解（DMD）及其变体构建有限维近似；引入时间延迟嵌入作为可观测量字典，克服标准DMD对驻波模式重构和非线性相互作用捕捉的局限；同时提出技术手段减轻传感器噪声对发光强度测量的影响。

Result: DMD-based模型能够将重构信号分解为特征分量，有效揭示不同运行模式的动力学特征；时间延迟嵌入显著改善了驻波模式的重构精度和对非线性相互作用的捕捉能力；噪声抑制技术提升了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于DMD和Koopman算子的方法为分析RDC中复杂的爆震波动力学提供了一种有效的线性化框架，能够分解不同运行模式的特征成分，有助于深入理解RDC的非线性行为。

Abstract: A Rotating Detonation Combustor (RDC) is a promising technology for increasing efficiency in propulsion and power generation applications. The dynamics of the RDC are governed by continuously propagating detonation waves within an annular combustion chamber. Multiple operating modes can be observed, including nonlinear interactions between counter-rotating waves and the emergence of standing wave patterns. Koopman operator theory provides a framework to globally linearize nonlinear dynamical systems by representing their evolution in the space of observables rather than states. In this work, finite-dimensional approximations of the Koopman operator are constructed using variants of Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) applied to high-speed video data capturing the natural flame luminosity of the detonation waves in the RDC at the Technical University (TU) Berlin. By introducing time-delay embeddings as a dictionary of observables, this approach overcomes the limitations of standard DMD methods, particularly for accurate reconstruction of standing wave patterns and for capturing nonlinear interactions. In addition, a technique is presented to mitigate the influence of sensor noise in the luminosity measurements. Finally, it is shown that the DMD-based models provide insight into the dynamics of different operating modes by decomposing the reconstructed signal into its characteristic features.

</details>


### [180] [Fully Convergent Projection-based Methods with Momentum under Nonconvex Geometric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22510)
*Matteo Lapucci,Diego Scuppa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在投影梯度法中集成动量项以加速非线性优化问题的收敛，同时保持收敛到强平稳性条件的理论保证，并通过数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性优化问题中常面临复杂、非凸但具有几何结构约束的难题。投影梯度法虽能收敛到强平稳性条件，但收敛速度较慢。动量项虽常用于加速优化，但如何在不破坏收敛保证的前提下将其集成到投影梯度框架中是一个尚未解决的问题。

Method: (1) 揭示了直接用一般下降方向替换负梯度在投影方法中的内在问题；(2) 提出了适用于投影前步骤的回溯机制，允许在方向中集成动量项；(3) 在无光滑性假设下保证收敛到Mordukhovich平稳点；(4) 当搜索方向在小步长时精确回归负梯度，可进一步收敛到Bouligand和Proximal平稳点。

Result: 在稀疏性和有界秩约束问题上进行数值测试，结果表明所提方法在计算上有效，能够利用动量项提供的额外信息加速收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的回溯机制成功将动量项集成到投影梯度法中，在不牺牲收敛保证的前提下提升了算法效率，为处理复杂非凸约束优化问题提供了实用且理论完备的方案。

Abstract: Nonlinear optimization problems with complicated, nonconvex, yet geometrically structured constraints can be tackled by projected-gradient methods: under weak regularity assumptions, these approaches were recently proved to possess convergence properties to the strongest stationarity conditions. In this work, we show how momentum terms, commonly used in nonlinear optimization to speed up the convergence process, can be integrated within this algorithmic framework without harming convergence guarantees. Preliminarily, we highlight an intrinsic issue induced by the direct replacement of the negative gradient with a general descent direction within the projected approach. Then, we present suitable backtracking mechanisms for the pre-projection step, allowing us to integrate momentum terms in the direction. By this technique, we can specifically ensure, without any smoothness assumptions, convergence to Mordukhovich stationarity as long as the base directions asymptotically revert to the negative gradient for small stepsizes; moreover, if the base search direction reverts exactly to the negative gradient for the smallest steps, the algorithm is proved to converge to Bouligand and Proximally stationary points, with and without (local) smoothness assumptions respectively. Finally, the proposed procedure is numerically tested on some classes of problems, namely, sparsity and bounded-rank constrained problems; the results indicate that the proposed method is computationally effective, taking advantage of the additional information provided by the momentum term.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.MF'></div>

# q-fin.MF [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Neilson's Weak vs. Strong Loss Aversion: A Characterization and a Generalized CPT-Utility Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22085)
*Symeon Vaidanis,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文对Neilson定义的规避概念进行了严格的理论分析，提出了广义CPT-效用函数，解决了标准CPT规范在满足Neilson型规避条件时的结构限制和不一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标风险决策中，需要超越风险中性和期望效用理论的局限，基于累积前景理论(CPT)的行为框架，对Neilson定义的规避概念进行严格的理论分析，并解决现有CPT规范的结构缺陷。

Method: 通过严格的数学理论分析，对Neilson的弱/强规避定义进行赌博解释和概念区分，考察Kobberling-Wakker效用函数及标准CPT规范，提出具有参考点依赖形状且更灵活的广义CPT-效用函数。

Result: 明确了强弱规避概念的趋同与分歧条件；揭示了标准CPT规范在满足Neilson型规避条件时的结构限制和不一致性；提出了广义CPT-效用函数。

Conclusion: 广义CPT-效用函数扩展了CPT的描述范围，为敏感性分析和基于效用函数优化提供了显式的函数形式，增强了行为决策模型的适用性。

Abstract: In multi-objective and multi-criteria decision-making under risk, especially in settings involving individual behavior, risk-aware analysis based on subjective evaluation has become increasingly important. Moving beyond risk-neutral modeling and the constraints of Expected Utility Theory (EUT), Cumulative Prospect Theory (CPT) provides a behaviorally grounded framework for capturing how individuals perceive and evaluate risky prospects. This paper conducts a rigorous theoretical analysis of Neilson's definitions of aversion. We provide a gamble-based interpretation, sharpen key conceptual distinctions, and make explicit the conditions under which the weak and strong notions coincide as well as when they diverge. Furthermore, we examine the Kobberling-Wakker utility function and related standard CPT specifications, highlighting structural limitations and inconsistencies that arise when these forms are required to satisfy Neilson-type aversion conditions. To address these issues, we propose a generalized CPT-utility function that retains the canonical reference-dependent shape while offering additional flexibility. This generalization extends the descriptive scope of CPT and provides an explicit functional form that is useful for sensitivity analysis and utility function-based optimization.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Prior laundering: learned priors with inherited, undetectable overconfidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21721)
*Ali Siahkoohi,Sina Alemohammad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文指出，在贝叶斯逆问题中使用学习生成先验时，若训练数据来自旧的重建结果（“先验洗钱”），则后验不确定性反映的并非真实数据信息，而是继承的旧先验信念，导致盲子空间上过度自信；唯一改进来自期望最大化的一步更新，但盲方向上的不确定性仍被冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震和医学成像中缺乏真实数据，只能使用旧重建档案训练生成先验，但这样得到的不确定性是否可靠？作者旨在揭示这种“先验洗钱”的根本问题。

Method: 理论分析表明，将旧后验在测量上平均等价于对旧正则化器进行一步期望最大化更新；实验上对比了扩散先验（档案训练 vs 真实数据训练）在盲子空间上的覆盖表现，以及标准化流在非线性地下水算子上的表现。

Result: 档案训练的先验在盲子空间上覆盖不足（欠覆盖），而真实数据训练的先验表现正常；单最佳档案甚至使盲可信区间宽度归零。

Conclusion: 建议在实际部署中明确报告哪些方向被测量解析，区分数据支持的置信度与通过管道继承的信念。

Abstract: Learned generative priors are increasingly used for ill-posed Bayesian inverse problems, their posterior uncertainty treated as earned from data. But training one requires truths, scarce in seismic and medical imaging, so the recourse is an archive of legacy reconstructions---prior laundering. Where the measurements are uninformative the posterior reverts to the prior, so the uncertainty reported there is the archive's, not the data's, and nothing in deployment reveals it: truths differing only on those directions induce identical data laws, and self-consistency checks such as simulation-based calibration pass whatever the prior believes. That belief has an exact source: averaging the legacy posterior over the measurements yields the old regularizer advanced a single expectation--maximization step---improved where the data resolve, frozen where they cannot. It is overconfident wherever the inherited belief is tighter than the truth. A single-best archive is worse, collapsing the blind credible interval to zero width. Deployed, a diffusion prior fit to the archive under-covers the operator's blind subspace, unlike a truth-trained control, and a normalizing flow does the same on a nonlinear groundwater operator. We recommend reporting which directions the measurements resolve, separating confidence the data support from belief inherited through the pipeline.

</details>


### [183] [Simulation-Based Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21843)
*Xinwei Shen,Diana Cai,Cheng Zhang,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于隐式似然函数（仅有模拟器可用）的经验贝叶斯方法——模拟驱动经验贝叶斯（SBEB），通过连接非参数EB与模拟驱动推断，利用观测数据、模拟样本和推理网络迭代优化先验，在多个科学模拟器和真实数据上提升了推断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验贝叶斯要求似然函数可显式计算，但许多科学应用中似然函数只能通过模拟器获得，限制了EB方法的应用范围。

Method: 提出SBEB方法，将非参数经验贝叶斯与模拟驱动推断相结合，利用观测数据、模拟器采样以及摊销推理网络，无需显式似然密度即可计算EB估计，并通过迭代方式不断优化EB先验使其逼近群体先验。

Result: 在多个科学模拟器和真实世界数据上的实验表明，SBEB相比使用固定先验的模拟驱动推断方法，推断准确性有显著提升。

Conclusion: SBEB成功将经验贝叶斯的优势拓展至隐式似然场景，为仅有模拟器可用的科学问题提供了一种更精准的推断框架。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) performs simultaneous inference across many related latent variables. Classical EB assumes that the likelihood p(x | z) is tractable. In many scientific applications, however, the likelihood is available only through a simulator. This paper develops EB for such implicit likelihoods. We introduce simulation-based empirical Bayes (SBEB), which connects nonparametric EB to simulation-based inference (SBI). SBEB computes EB estimates without an explicit density by using the observed data, simulator samples, and an amortized inference network. SBEB iteratively refines the fitted EB prior toward the population prior. With several scientific simulators and real-world data, we demonstrate that SBEB improves accuracy over SBI with a fixed prior.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient Online LLM Watermark Detection via Rao-Blackwellized E-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21958)
*Lu Luo,Dandan Mo,Chengdong Xu,Ting Li,Jinhan Xie,Huiqiong Li,Niansheng Tang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Rao-Blackwell化e-process的在线水印检测框架，支持任意停止的流式文本检测，并理论上证明了其随时有效的第一类错误控制和渐近一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型文本水印检测方法多为固定视界（fixed-horizon）程序，无法在流式生成过程中进行有效的提前停止（early stopping），缺乏在线检测能力。

Method: 基于Rao-Blackwell化的e-process构建在线水印检测框架；将Gumbel-max水印的token级依赖检验转化为枢轴量驱动的序列检验问题；设计递进式token级证据更新机制，无需存储完整历史序列。

Result: 理论上证明了在任意可选停止条件下具备随时有效的第一类错误控制，并建立了水印存在下渐近正对数增长的一致性；模拟和真实LLM生成文本实验验证了在线检测的高效性。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了LLM水印的在线检测，兼具严格的随时有效统计保证和实际可用性，为流式生成场景下的AI文本检测提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, reliable and efficient mechanisms for distinguishing AI-generated text from human-written content have become essential. Statistical watermarking has emerged as a promising solution, yet most existing methods are typically fixed-horizon procedures, precluding valid early stopping in streaming generation. In this paper, we develop an efficient online watermark detection framework with anytime-valid inference based on Rao-Blackwellized e-processes, enabling recursive token-level evidence updates without storing the full history. In particular, we instantiate the framework for the Gumbel-max watermark and reduce the original token-level dependence testing problem to a pivot-induced sequential testing problem with an explicit null distribution. Theoretically, we prove anytime-valid Type I error control under arbitrary optional stopping and establish positive asymptotic log-growth under watermarking, implying consistency of the proposed stopping rules. Simulations and experiments on real LLM-generated text demonstrate efficient online detection with rigorous anytime-valid guarantees.

</details>


### [185] [Convergence analysis of a family of Zermelo-type iterations for the Bradley--Terry model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22221)
*Ruijian Han,Ding Lu,Yiming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Newman提出的Zermelo型固定点迭代算法族（参数α）在Bradley-Terry模型中的局部收敛性。通过同步和异步更新的雅可比矩阵谱分析，发现异步更新下α=0收敛最快且单调递减，而同步更新下α<1可能不收敛。理论分析和实验验证了加速源于异步更新而非单纯的参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zermelo算法在BT模型中收敛慢，Newman提出的α参数化家族中α=0经验上收敛更快，但其加速机制尚不明确，本文旨在从理论上解释这一现象。

Method: 通过推导同步和异步更新下的局部收敛因子闭式表达式，利用雅可比矩阵谱分析研究收敛因子对α的依赖关系，并在群体BT模型和一致有序二分比较图上进行理论分析。

Result: 同步更新下α<1时算法可能不收敛，局部收敛因子关于α呈拟凸；异步更新下总是局部收敛，且收敛因子关于α单调递增，证明α=0最优；给出了群体收敛因子的渐近近似结果。

Conclusion: α=0的加速不仅源于参数选择，更关键的是异步更新的使用。理论分析与合成数据及真实数据实验一致。

Abstract: Zermelo's algorithm is a classical method for computing the maximum likelihood estimator in the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, but its convergence can be slow in practice. To accelerate computation, Newman introduced a family of Zermelo-type fixed-point iterations parameterized by $α$, with Zermelo's algorithm recovered at $α=1$. Empirical evidence suggests that the choice $α=0$ often converges substantially faster, making it a promising alternative, yet the mechanism underlying this acceleration remains elusive. This paper provides theoretical insight into this phenomenon through a systematic local convergence analysis. We derive closed-form expressions for local convergence factors under synchronous and asynchronous updates and analyze their dependence on $α$ via spectral analysis of the associated Jacobian matrices. For synchronous updates, we show that the algorithm may fail to converge when $α<1$, and its local convergence factor is quasi-convex in $α$ under the population BT model. In contrast, asynchronous updates are always locally convergent, and their local convergence factor is provably monotonically increasing in $α$ under the population BT model of consistently ordered bipartite comparison graphs, establishing the optimality of $α=0$ in this setting. We further establish asymptotic approximation results for the population convergence factors under the BT model, justifying their practical relevance. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the theory. Our analysis complements existing convergence results and shows that the acceleration of $α=0$ arises not only from the parameter choice but, more importantly, from the use of asynchronous updates.

</details>


### [186] [CausalForge: A Formally Grounded, Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Automated Research in Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22511)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalForge框架，通过结合Lean证明助手和LLM实现因果推断领域自动化理论研究，包含形式化库Causalean和自改进代理管线CausalSmith，并通过语句审计确保形式化定理与非正式声明的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM审稿人不可靠，无法区分真假论文（检测率接近随机水平），而自动化理论研究不仅需要生成候选结果，还需要可靠的评估机制。因此需要一个形式化验证框架来保证理论结果的正确性。

Method: CausalForge由两部分组成：(1) Causalean——在LLM辅助和人工设计审查下构建的包含7,035个机器检查声明的因果推断Lean形式化库；(2) CausalSmith——自改进代理管线，自动选择研究主题、提出结果、形式化声明、构造证明，并输出供人工审查的产物。管线在核验证之外增加了语句审计环节，比较形式化定理与其对应的非正式声明。

Result: 通过完成的自主研究运行产生的产物对系统进行评估。代码、形式化库和运行记录已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 机器检查证明只能确保形式化声明从假设中推导得出，不能保证该声明忠实表达了预期的科学主张，因此语句审计是必要的补充。CausalForge为自动化理论研究提供了更可靠的形式化验证路径。

Abstract: Automating theoretical research is constrained not only by the generation of candidate results, but also by their reliable evaluation. A common approach is to close the research loop with a large language model (LLM) reviewer. However, such reviewers remain empirically unreliable: they may accept fabricated papers and detect them at rates close to chance (Bad Scientist, 2025). We present CausalForge, a framework for automated theoretical research in causal inference grounded in the Lean proof assistant. CausalForge combines Causalean, a foundational Lean library for causal inference containing 7,035 machine-checked declarations developed with language-model assistance under human design and review, with CausalSmith, a self-improving agentic pipeline that selects research topics, proposes results, formalizes statements, constructs proofs, and presents the resulting artifacts for human inspection. Because a machine-checked proof establishes only that a formal statement follows from its assumptions, not that the statement faithfully captures the intended scientific claim, the pipeline augments kernel verification with a statement audit that compares each formal theorem against the informal claim it is intended to express. We evaluate the system using artifacts produced by completed autonomous research runs. The source code, formal library, and run records are available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/CausalForge.

</details>
